- **ai-coding-ok**: PDCA memory loop for Claude Code — prevents "AI fixed bug X and broke feature Y" across iterations. Installs a three-tier memory system in your project (project-memory, decisions-log, task-history) and enforces a closed PDCA loop on every task: Plan (read memory before coding), Do, Check, Act (write memory back after).
